<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have all the data I need. Let me note:

1. VLA-only SRs are stored as fractions (0.995, 1.0) → 99.5%, 100%
2. CRM SRs are stored as percentages (89, 87, 88)
3. Adaptive SRs are stored as fractions (0.855, 0.85) → 85.5%, 85.0%

Wait, that can't be right. If VLA-only on Object is ~99.5% and CRM is ~88%, that means CRM is degrading performance from VLA-only by ~11pp. And Adaptive is degrading by ~14pp.


This actually makes sense though—π0.5 is near-perfect on the easy Object benchmark, so any intervention like CRM corrections would likely hurt more than help when the base model is already at ceiling. Adaptive Conformal performs even worse than both, which suggests the trigger rate might be the issue here.

The adaptive trigger is only firing 4% of the time, yet it still causes a 14-point drop compared to VLA-only. That's a significant penalty for such minimal intervention. Let me verify the CRM numbers—if VLA-only is 99.5% and CRM drops to 89%, that's an 11-point degradation, which is already substantial. The always-on CRM might be applying corrections too aggressively on a task where the base model is already performing near-optimally.

Even with the adaptive approach at τ=0.25, which only triggers corrections 4% of the time, performance still falls to 85%. This suggests that even occasional corrections with the conformal thresholding are harmful on Object tasks where the VLA baseline is already near-ceiling. I should note that one seed (s44) failed for the adaptive run, so I'm only working with 2 valid seeds here.

The key takeaway is that this experiment shows a negative result—adaptive conformal actually hurts performance compared to the VLA-only baseline (85% vs 99.5%). However, the adaptive method is designed for scenarios where the base model is weak, not where it's already near-perfect like on Object tasks.
```

**来源：log + JSON（cqa1-32758）**

| Task | SR |
|------|-----|

| salad_dressing | 95% |
| milk | 95% |
| bbq_sauce | 90% |
| tomato_sauce | 85% |
| chocolate_pudding | 85% |
| orange_juice | 85% |
| butter | 80% |
| cream_cheese | 70% |

无 OOM / CUDA error，EXIT_CODE=0，实验健康。

---

## 同组对比（Registry）

### Adaptive Conformal on Object（τ=0.25, K=2, norm=0.2）

| Seed | SR | Trigger Rate | Source |
|------|-----|-------------|--------|
| s42 | 85.5% | 3.68% | pi05-adaptive-object-s42-cqa1 |
| **s43** | **85.0%** | **4.0%** | **本次实验** |
| s44 | — | — | failed (pi05-adaptive-object-s44-cqa1-r2) |

**2-seed 均值：85.25% ± 0.35%**（s42+s43），高度一致。s44 无有效数据。

### 对比 Baseline（Registry）

| Method | s42 | s43 | s44 | Mean±Std |
|--------|-----|-----|-----|----------|
| **VLA-only** | 99.5% | 100% | 99.5% | **99.7% ± 0.3%** |
| **CRM (always-on)** | 89% | 87% | 88% | **88.0% ± 1.0%** |
| **Adaptive Conformal** | 85.5% | 85.0% | — | **85.25% ± 0.35%** |

---

## Conclusion: **Negative**

π0.5 VLA-only 在 Object 上接近 ceiling（99.7%）。任何 correction 干预都在 degrading performance：

- CRM always-on：-11.7pp（99.7→88.0）
- Adaptive Conformal：-14.4pp（99.7→85.25）

Adaptive 的 trigger rate 仅 4%（conformal 阈值正确地保守），但触发时的 corrections（norm=0.2 打满 cap）在 near-expert VLA 上仍然有害。Adaptive 甚至比 always-on CRM 低 2.75pp，说明 conformal 触发的时机选择可能并不优于随机——在 VLA 已经近乎完美的场景下，"何时 correct" 和 "是否 correct" 同样不成立。

**对 Table 2 的意义：** Object suite 确认了预期——Adaptive Conformal 在 VLA 强势场景下无正向收益。该方法的价值需要在 VLA 较弱的 suite（Spatial、Long）上体现。s42/s43 两个 seed 高度一致，结果可信。s44 仍需补跑才能完成 3-seed 统计。
